Table headers are `sticky top-0`.

## 2026-09-30 — Deal lifecycle R1–R4 shipped; migrations 212 + 213 live (`b99c8e01`, `e42a9caa`; the spec blueprint was deleted 2026-10-25, its locked decisions are kept below)
- **R1 scheduler fix:** `AsyncIOScheduler(job_defaults={"misfire_grace_time": 60, "coalesce": True, "max_instances": 1})`. Before, the 1 s default grace skipped almost every timed job (intake-staleness-sweep 6 ran / 8,514 missed in 30 days). Verified live: 385/385 success in one hour after the deploy.
- **212_intake_deal_lifecycle.sql (applied live before deploy):** `intake_sessions` gains `last_activity_at` (backfilled from `created_at`), `razorpay_payment_link_id`, `refund_needed`, `extra_payment_ids`.
- **213_intake_one_open_per_lead.sql (applied live 2026-09-30 16:4x UTC, after the deploy):** partial unique index on (tenant_id, lead_id) WHERE status NOT IN ('paid','cancelled','resolved'). Before applying it, 0 leads had two open rows. `_create_session` turns a 23505 into "return the existing open session".
- **Link expiry no longer cancels the deal.** It clears the link and the deal stays open; the AI asks "continue or start new?" when the lead comes back. Only the per-tenant idle sweep closes an unpaid deal, after `deal_idle_close_days` (default 30, range 2–90, operator console "Unpaid Deals"). The lost reason is "No reply from the customer (idle close)".
- **Locked decisions (D1–D8, from the deleted blueprint):** D1 idle-close clock per tenant, 2–90 days, default 30. D2 a 24 h link expiring kills only the link, never the deal. D3 `last_activity_at` resets only on a lead message or real progress (package picked, details saved, link created, paid), never on our outbound or link expiry. D4 ask "continue or something else?" only if the lead was away ≥ 24 h and intent is unclear; the LLM judges meaning, no keyword lists; `close_deal` needs an explicit decline. D5 ship bar: 0 wrong closes, 0 dead/old-price links, ≥ 90% right actions. D6 on start-fresh, show old details for the lead to confirm, never reuse silently. D7 prevent double payment (cancel the old Razorpay link), catch it (`refund_needed` + staff WhatsApp); a human refunds, no auto-refund. D8 rollout R1 scheduler → R2 money safety → R3 lifecycle + AI → R4 board tags.
- **User decisions:** (a) no 48 h block for paid customers: `select_offering` with `new_booking: true` starts a fresh session next to a paid one at once. A paid session is never re-pointed. (b) If the price rises between links, `create_payment_link` refuses until the lead says yes (`customer_agreed_new_price: true`). A price drop is only mentioned. (c) Shipped with the D5 eval at 80–84% right actions, against a 90% bar. There were 0 wrong closes and 0 dead or old-price links. The evals used the Astro Tamil key, with explicit user OK for testing only.

### 2026-10-01 — Bind configured WhatsApp choices to their data

- **Problem:** Astro Tamil's generated package labels bypassed saved short labels and became a truncated list; detail buttons depended on English label matching in AI wording.
- **Decision:** `offer_choices` accepts exact offering IDs or a detail field key. Code supplies saved menus; legacy recognizable package labels resolve to a unique sibling offering level. Detail IDs bind the session, field and canonical option; taps save before the model runs, with guards for stale/paid sessions and configuration changes. Explicit field references also permit corrections to answered details. Oversized field IDs use full text choices instead of unbound truncated menus.
- **214_messages_interactive_id.sql — applied live:** nullable `messages.interactive_id` records incoming WhatsApp selections. RLS remains enabled. Bound detail rows are excluded from subsequent extraction and reply history; existing NULL rows cannot be retrospectively identified. Migration must precede the backend deployment. A rollback first removes the code dependency; column removal, if desired, belongs in a separate forward migration.
- **Validation:** regression tests cover the screenshot, Tanglish Male/Female questions, out-of-order answers, corrections, stale taps, canonical saving and WhatsApp format limits. No live WhatsApp messages or paid model evaluations were sent. Backend changes have not been deployed.

### 2026-10-03 — Call Notes becomes Call Review (admin), notes record their author, CI backend fix (`23670426`, `8eea400e`, `d9920815`)

- **Call Review:** owners and anyone with `team.manage` get a new page at the same URL `/dashboard/notes` (so `telecalling.notes` permissions are untouched); telecallers keep the old notes page for now (user choice). Name chosen by the user from Call Review / Conversations / Coaching. Mockup: https://claude.ai/artifact/1P4wcj7MxUwiEPpspHTp4h
- **Server endpoint, not browser filtering** (user choice A over B): `GET /api/v1/call-review/leads?start&end&caller_id` returns per-lead totals; the page sums them for the KPI strip so the numbers always match the visible filters (telecaller, dates, search). Tabs only narrow the list. A separate score filter from the mockup was dropped for the same reason.
- **Note author comes from the signed-in user, never the request body.** `CreateNote.caller_id` was removed; `create_lead_note` stamps `ctx["caller_id"]`. Before this no client ever sent it, so every note had a null author.
- **Dialer Recent tab:** rows show only lead name + "by <telecaller>"; time/length/outcome/score moved into the expanded row. Callers already only see their own calls server-side (`calls.py` `/recent`).
- **CI backend tests had failed on nearly every push since 2026-09-30** — one test, `test_latest_version_reads_version_json_and_caches`. Root cause in `_latest_sim_app_version`: see subsystem-notes (SIM call tracking). CI green again on `d9920815`.

### 2026-10-03 — Call Review: filters, manual-only notes, full-bleed layout (`ae62ba63` and follow-ups)

- **Notes rail shows only typed notes** (no `AI Summary:` rows, no empty ones) with "25 Sep 26, 14:30" timestamps. **`call_ai_pipeline.py` stopped inserting `AI Summary:` notes** — the next step is already on `call_logs.ai_summary.next_action`, shown per call. Seven legacy rows (4 linked to a call, 3 from 19 Sep unlinked) were left in `lead_notes`; the Supabase MCP declined the delete, so the user must run `delete from lead_notes where content like 'AI Summary:%' and caller_id is null;` if wanted. They still appear in the dialer/conversation notes lists until then.
- **Filters added:** score band (80+, 60–79, under 60, not scored — by the lead's average over scored calls) and segment, plus a Clear button. A sort dropdown was built and then removed at the user's request; the list keeps server order.
- **"Author not recorded"** = note with null `caller_id` (saved before authors were recorded). New notes get the signed-in caller's name.

### 2026-10-03 — Auto-Messages: event-triggered WhatsApp templates (design: `docs/designs/auto-messages.md`)

- **What:** a number + event (`interested` / `signed_up` / `purchased`) + product arrives from a copy-paste website form (`GET /api/v1/auto-messages/in/{token}/form.js`), an API/Zapier/billing webhook (`POST /api/v1/auto-messages/in/{token}`) or the shop-counter quick-add screen; Aira sends the matching approved template once. Dashboard page `/dashboard/auto-messages`. Researched WATI / AiSensy / Interakt first: they leave "which product -> which template" to the client's developer or Zapier; Aira matches against Products (name + `catalog_items.aliases`) with no code.
- **Rules:** one per (tenant, event, product); `catalog_item_id NULL` = the event's default for unmatched/unknown products. Product rule beats default. Variables fill from first name / product / price / page URL / any extra field / fixed text, each with a fallback (Interakt pattern). Image headers use the product's first catalog photo; dynamic URL-button suffix is URL-encoded.
- **Never sends twice:** same phone + event + product within 24 h is logged `skipped/duplicate`. Delayed rows are sent by the `auto-messages` scheduler job (1 min); a `queued -> sending` conditional update claims each row. Rows stuck in `sending` 15 min are marked `failed/interrupted`, never re-sent.
- **Public intake hardening:** the token is effectively public (it sits in the website snippet), so it can only create leads and send the tenant's own templates. Rate limit 10 / 10 min per IP (CF/True-Client-IP, else rightmost X-Forwarded-For) and 1000 / h per tenant, honeypot field, and any link in a customer-supplied name/product/extra field is dropped before it reaches a template (phishing via the tenant's own template).
- **New lead sources** `website`, `api`, `store` (opt_in_source `website_form`, `api`, `offline_event`). Added to `schemas.SourceType` (lead detail would 500 otherwise) and the analytics breakdowns. Deliberately NOT added to `INBOUND_SOURCES` or the dialer's `_MESSAGE_LEAD_SOURCES` — open product question.
- **215_auto_messages.sql — applied live 2026-10-03 by the user via the SQL editor** (the Supabase MCP connector refused `apply_migration`). Adds `auto_message_rules`, `auto_message_sends` (log + queue), `catalog_items.aliases`, widens the two `leads` source CHECKs. RLS on, member SELECT only; writes are service-role. Must precede the backend deploy.
- **2026-10-05 — products removed (user-decided).** One message per event, sent to everyone; no product picker on the website form, counter or rule editor, no catalog matching, aliases or product photo (image headers use the template's own image). Variable sources are now first name / full name / page URL / phone / extra field / fixed text (`product_url` renamed `page_url`); a `product` field sent by an app is just an extra field. Duplicate = same phone + event within 24 h. **216_auto_messages_drop_products.sql** drops `auto_message_rules.catalog_item_id` (unique index now `(tenant_id, event)`), `auto_message_sends.product_raw/catalog_item_id` and `catalog_items.aliases` — apply AFTER the backend that stops reading them is deployed (the reverse of 215).

### 2026-10-04 — One WhatsApp menu rule, URL buttons, unverified-link guard (`50859f73`, `38cdc740` — local, NOT pushed)

- **Rule (user-decided):** 2 options = reply buttons, 3–10 = list, one https link = URL button (`cta_url`). Applies to the AI-reply path and package menus (`intake._tap_mode`). Before: up to 3 buttons.
- **Why:** 2026-10-01 a package list ("Edhu venum-nu sollunga", no `?`) and a Tanglish confirm question went out as plain text — `_package_menu` needed a `?` and the yes/no regex missed "correct-ah / maathano". Diagnosed from `messages` rows: no `[Title]` tags and no `•` bullets = no menu was ever attempted (a Meta rejection leaves `•` bullets).
- **List bar label** follows the customer's language: model's `list_label` (offer_choices) or classifier label → script/Tanglish table (`choices.LIST_LABELS`) → "Options". Link labels: `choices.LINK_LABELS`. Table translations were written by the model, not a native speaker — review pending.
- **Safety net:** a reply with a `?` that matched no pattern gets one small timed model call (`CHOICE_CLASSIFY_TIMEOUT` 2.5 s, fails open to plain text). Statements, replies with a menu/payment link/quote, handovers skip it.
- **Link guard (`38cdc740`):** a link in the model's reply must appear in the prompt, business details, catalog or config, else it is removed and the model is told on a retry. Earlier assistant replies are NOT trusted. Raw `{{PLACEHOLDER}}` is stripped. Trigger: the AI invented `play.google.com/...id=com.astrotamil.app` (404) because knowledge said "use {{PLAY_STORE_LINK}}" and nothing fills it.
- **Not done:** `intake.py` tap handling, auto-messages, broadcasts still use their own senders. No `menu_status` column (tags/bullets already encode the outcome). A reply with both choices and a link keeps the link as text inside the menu body (Meta can't combine reply buttons with a URL button).

### 2026-10-05 — Template builder enforces Meta's rules (`526b23ff`, `48ddae87` pushed; `e2f87419` local, NOT pushed)

- **Rule (user-decided):** if a Meta rule can be fixed for the client, fix it as they type and show a small green note; block Continue/Submit only for what they must fix, with a "N things to fix" pill that jumps to the first field; the variables-to-length ratio is a calm grey tip only (Meta publishes no number; providers report a floor of 3 words per variable + 1). Keep the builder's existing look.
- **Rules live in two mirrored files:** `frontend/app/dashboard/templates/template-rules.ts` (live) and `backend/app/services/template_rules.py` (server refuses with a 400 before calling Meta). Change one, change both.
- **Sample values are required** (Meta: "you must include sample variable values"). The client types them; the old invented "Rajan Kumar"/"Sample text" (`_extract_variable_examples`) is deleted. Not stored — the edit page asks again on resubmit.
- **Delete is Meta-first by `hsm_id`** (only that language; by name Meta deletes every language). Meta refuses → 502, row kept so the client can retry; "not found" on Meta → local delete. Meta: an approved template's name can't be reused for 30 days.
- **Media header upload uses `env_settings.meta_app_id`** (Aira's app, the one clients connect through), with a tenant `meta_app_id` app_setting as override. Before, it read only the tenant setting, which no screen sets → every upload failed.

### 2026-10-05 — Aira ID on templates + signed partner sends for AstroTamil (replaces Bulkwise)

- **What:** every WhatsApp template gets a 6-digit **Aira ID** (`message_templates.short_code`, unique per tenant), shown with tap-to-copy on the Templates page. AstroTamil's Django admin stores this code instead of a Bulkwise template id and calls `POST /api/v1/intake/partner/send-template` (or `/partner/send-text`), signed with the tenant's `astro_bridge_secret`. Wire contract: subsystem-notes.md → AstroTamil consultation bridge, item C.
- **No lead is created** for an unknown number: app users must not enter Aira's assignment / re-engagement automations. Existing leads get the send logged in their chat with `reply_source='automation'` (the CHECK constraint has no partner value; auto-messages uses the same one).
- **217_message_templates_short_code.sql — applied live (verified 2026-10-08: `message_templates.short_code` exists, NOT NULL).** Adds `short_code`, a `BEFORE INSERT` trigger (`message_templates_set_short_code`) that draws a random unused 100000–999999 per tenant under a per-tenant advisory lock, backfills existing rows, then a unique index on (tenant_id, short_code) and NOT NULL. No Python insert path sets the column. Must precede the backend deploy (the send-template lookup filters on `short_code`).

- **2026-10-06 — Partner send drops extra template variables.** AstroTamil's Django always sends `[customer name, service name, "AstroTamil"]` for `{{1}}`/`{{2}}`/`{{3}}` and cannot know how many placeholders a given template uses, so `partner_send_template` trims the list to the template's count instead of returning `variables_mismatch`; too few values is still 400. Lets the client write templates with none, one or both placeholders without any per-template config.

- **2026-10-06 — Developer page in the client dashboard.** Aira is multi-vendor; a partner's developer needs the integration contract without asking us. `/dashboard/developer` documents the partner send API, signing, Aira IDs, errors and callbacks, with the tenant's own non-secret identifiers filled in from `GET /api/v1/intake/partner/config`. Secrets stay ops-issued (operator console) and are only reported as set/not set, in line with the 2026-08-23 decision not to expose bridge credentials client-side.

### 2026-10-07 — Private Send: Aira's rules, the client's own server sends (merged to main; built on branch `feat/private-send`)

- **What:** for clients (banks, private firms) whose lead data may not reach Aira. The client's server runs the open-source plug-in (`sdk/python` → `aira-private-send`, `sdk/node` → `aira-private-send`), downloads an Ed25519-signed bundle (enabled `auto_message_rules` + APPROVED templates + cap) from `GET /api/v1/private-send/bundle`, sends to Meta with the client's own System User token, and reports count-only daily totals to `POST /api/v1/private-send/usage`. Contract: `sdk/spec/CONTRACT.md`; client guide: `docs/private-send.md`.
- **Why not "send it to us and we delete it":** the client cannot verify deletion; with the plug-in, Aira never receives a name or number (the `/usage` model is `extra="forbid"`).
- **Operator owns it:** on/off (feature `private_send`, hidden from the client cart — `OPERATOR_ONLY_FEATURES` in `routes/subscriptions.py`), monthly cap, license keys (shown once, sha256 stored), reply mode, offline grace. Rules stay on the client's Auto Messages page.
- **218_private_send.sql — applied live 2026-10-07 via the Supabase MCP (`apply_migration`), user-approved; verified after: RLS on, anon/authenticated have no table or RPC access.** `private_send_keys`, `private_send_usage`, `private_send_meta_daily`, RPC `record_private_send_usage` (GREATEST upsert, returns billable growth), metric `private_send_message`, catalog row. RLS on, all grants revoked from anon/authenticated, RPC execute for service_role only. Verified 2026-10-07 on a throwaway local Postgres 16 with Supabase-style default grants: applies and re-applies cleanly, billing cases pass, 3×20 concurrent reports billed exactly once. Must precede the backend deploy; also needs env `PRIVATE_SEND_SIGNING_KEY` (base64 32-byte Ed25519 seed) on Render or `/bundle` returns 503.
- **Not live-verified (needs a spare WhatsApp number on the UI test tenant):** a client System User token sending on a WABA Aira also manages, and Aira keeping template/analytics access after `DELETE subscribed_apps`.
- **2026-10-07 end-to-end on Astro Tamil (user-approved, option A = Aira's stored token):** local safe-mode backend + throwaway signing key; Python and Node plug-ins each sent `astro_reply_ready` to the user's test phone (Meta returned message ids), 24 h duplicate skip worked, revoked key → `LicenseError revoked_key`. Phone's leads/messages unchanged (2/62), 0 new rows. Everything reverted after (rule deleted, feature off, keys/usage/meta rows/counter deleted). Two findings below in subsystem-notes (Private Send).

### 2026-10-07 — Per-template variable map for the WhatsApp alerts
- **Why:** escalation and hot-lead alerts filled `{{1}}`… in a fixed order, so a client whose template has one placeholder for the phone got the lead's name.
- **Decision:** the map lives on the template (`message_templates.variable_map`), editable after approval from the template page; no map = unchanged behaviour. 14 fields: the 6 existing values plus assigned agent, enquiry date, last message from lead, business name, alert date & time, collected detail (any `collected_data` key), channel, last call status. User dropped lead score, first name and fixed text.
- **Scope (user choice):** the two alerts only — not the marketplace welcome, not partner send; flows with their own per-send mapping untouched.
- **219_message_templates_variable_map.sql — APPLIED to live 2026-10-07.** Nullable `jsonb` column; must precede the backend deploy (alert queries select it). 218 is taken by `feat/private-send`.

- **What:** commits `a6013229` (user-facing copy), `994ed214` (Ad Performance export header), `2fa366b1` (paths, backend text, SDK and kit — 143 files), `3929f215` (leftover visible text in Private Send, template and operator screens).
- **Status:** dashboard, operator and Private Send screens were renamed. NOT verified as intentional: `frontend/app/page.tsx` (landing page) and its `hello@aira.ai` mailto still say Aira, and ~65 `aira` word hits remain in frontend `.tsx` (many are internal identifiers like `sender: "aira"` / `chat-bubble-aira`). Ask Prem before renaming the landing page or the email.

### 2026-10-07 — Dashboard analytics RPCs retry a dropped connection
- **Why:** `_RetryTransport` (db/supabase.py) replays only GET/HEAD/OPTIONS because a dropped write may already have landed — but postgrest sends an RPC as a POST, so a "Server disconnected" on the four read-only analytics RPCs became a 500 and an "unavailable" card on the tenant's dashboard.
- **Decision:** `execute_with_retry_async()` (next to `execute_with_retry()` in `app/utils/db_retry.py`) wraps `analytics_daily_messages`, `analytics_period_money`, `analytics_response_times`, `analytics_daily_returning_ad_leads` (commit `dbf5491d`, test `tests/test_analytics_overview_rpc_retry.py`). Writes still raise. Rule: only wrap an RPC in a retry if it is read-only.

### 2026-10-07 — AppVersionTests cache reset made deterministic on fresh CI runners
- **Why:** the tests set `_sim_app_version_cache` `at=0.0` to force a cache miss, but the cache counts as fresh while `time.monotonic() - at < 600`; on a CI runner booted under 10 minutes ago, `monotonic()` is still below 600, so `0.0` looked fresh and the mocked HTTP call never ran.
- **Decision:** use `at=float("-inf")` (always older than the TTL). Commit `b7f4138c` (`backend/tests/test_call_feedback_gate.py`), merged as `b806b5cd`. Any new test that forces a TTL cache miss should do the same, never `0.0`.

Migration index extended to 120–219. Stale backlog items pruned, `identity.md` team line corrected, design-system and operator-run facts moved out of personal memory into `identity.md`.

### 2026-10-08 — Own Roles toggles for Deals, Meta Ads, Auto-Messages, Anril Brain, Services
- **Why:** the five pages rode on other pages' keys (Deals on `leads.*`, Meta Ads on `inbound_leads.view`, Auto-Messages on `settings.*` + `leads.manage`, Brain on `knowledge.*`, Services on `settings.*`/`catalog.*`), so they had no row in Roles and ticking Segments silently opened Deals.
- **Decision (user choice):** new keys `deals.*`, `meta_ads.*`, `auto_messages.*`, `brain.view`, `services.*`, enforced on the backend routes, sidebar, More menu and page guards. Brain is read-only in Roles — its edit actions write the Knowledge Base, so they still need `knowledge.manage`. Intake config (the Services page's data) accepts `services.*` or `settings.*` via the new `require_any_permission`. Meta Ads ad endpoints had no permission check before; now they do. Auto-Messages Write covers both the rules and the shop counter (the counter used to need only `leads.manage`; user was offered a split, undecided).
- **220_rbac_new_page_permissions.sql — applied live by the user** (verified 2026-10-08: all 9 `tenant_roles` rows carry `deals.view`). Additive backfill: each new key goes to every role that already reached the page. Must precede the backend deploy or those users lose the five pages. Merge `c48da128`, pushed to `main`.

### 2026-10-07 — Auto Messages v2: one in-place page, custom events, quiet hours
- **Why:** usage was zero (no tenant had a rule), so the page could be reshaped freely; events were locked to three and the editor was a pop-up.
- **Decision:** shop counter and the Zapier/Pabbly card removed (the event link lives on the Developer page now); clients add their own events (≤ 20 per tenant, code never changes); quiet hours cover Marketing templates only (21:00–09:00 IST wait for 09:00, Utility sends any time); the editor opens in place on the event row; the event link, "Send from your server" keys, public key and reply mode moved to the Developer page; SDK renamed `anril-connector` v1.1.0 (Python module `anril_connector`, class `AnrilPrivateSend` kept). Follow-up sequences (day-2 nudge) deferred until a client is live and asks.
- **221_auto_message_events.sql — applied live (confirmed 2026-10-08 via Supabase `list_migrations`, version `20261008072757`).** It had to run BEFORE the backend deploy. `GET /auto-messages/rules` calls the `auto_message_sent_events` RPC (service_role only), so it errors without it. Creates `auto_message_events` (custom events; RLS select-only for members), drops the event CHECK on `auto_message_rules` (and `auto_message_sends` if present), adds an index on `auto_message_sends (tenant_id, status, event)`. Additive, so safe to apply first.

### 2026-10-08 — Auto Messages: one engine, instant, no checks
- **Why:** the user chose AstroTamil-friendly simplicity: one engine, every message instant, no hidden skips (a customer who got nothing with no visible reason was the worst outcome). If a check is ever needed it comes back as opt-in configuration, never as a silent default.
- **Decision:**
  - **Removed everywhere** (hosted engine `services/auto_messages.py`, Private Send bundle `services/private_send.py`, both SDKs, Partner API): opt-out check, 24-hour no-repeat, quiet hours (Marketing 21:00-09:00) and the per-rule wait. `auto_message_rules.delay_minutes` stays as a column, always 0, and the API no longer accepts it (ignored if sent). The bundle has no `quiet_hours` and rules have no `delay_minutes`; plug-ins ignore both if an older backend sends them. The only skip left is `no_rule`.
  - **SDK:** `track()` inserts a queued row and delivers it in the same call; `run_due` is retry-only (connect failure, Meta 429/503; retry after 1 min, `failed/gave_up` after 1 h); `track` returns `queued/anril_unreachable` instead of raising `BundleUnavailable`. Contract: `sdk/spec/CONTRACT.md`.
  - **Private Send `reply_mode` now defaults to `aira`** (replies reach Anril's inbox); `client` is opt-in. `get_reply_mode` in `services/private_send.py` is the only reader.
  - **Partner API event mode:** `POST /api/v1/intake/partner/send-template` takes exactly one of `template_code` (unchanged, AstroTamil) or the new `event` (+ `phone`, `name?`, `extra?`, `reference?`); event mode uses the tenant's Auto Messages rule, sends at once and returns 409 `private_send_on` while the tenant sends from its own server. Code: `services/partner_events.py`, `services/intake.py`. Never creates a lead; only the website form / hosted ingest does, and their `private_send_on` guard stays.
  - **Partner sends are logged:** every send-template call, sent or failed, writes one `auto_message_sends` row with `source='partner'` (`event` NULL in `template_code` mode, `reference` in `extra`). A log failure never blocks the send; `send-text` is untouched. Auto Messages Activity shows them as "Sent by your app · Partner API".
  - **Developer page:** `DoorChooser` has two rows, "Anril sends for you" (send-template, with a "Send an event instead" subsection, `EventModeSubsection.tsx`) and "Your server sends". The old `EventLinkSection` / `EventTryIt` were removed.
- **222_partner_sends_log.sql — applied live (confirmed 2026-10-08 via Supabase `list_migrations`, version `20261008072804`); was dry-run verified on a throwaway Postgres first.** Widens the `auto_message_sends.source` CHECK to include `'partner'`, makes `event` nullable, and redefines `auto_message_sent_events()` to skip NULL events. Additive, safe to apply before the backend deploy. **Apply 221, then 222, before the backend deploy** (221 is also not applied live yet). The numbers moved up one because keerthi-sarav's `220_rbac_new_page_permissions.sql` (applied live) took 220.
- **Per-account partner send mode:** `POST /partner/send-template` takes a `template_code` OR an `event` depending on the account's setting `partner_send_mode` (`template` default, `event`), chosen by an admin on the Developer page (`GET`/`PUT /api/v1/intake/partner/send-mode`, `settings.view`/`settings.manage`, no migration). The other shape is 400 `wrong_mode`, nothing sent or logged; the old "both fields → invalid_request" is gone. Why: one way per account removes per-call confusion about which shape a call should have, and AstroTamil has no row so it stays on `template` with nothing to change in their app. Code: `services/partner_send_mode.py`, `services/intake.py`, `developer/SendModePicker.tsx`.

- **Why:** the product had outgrown living under Bloom Matrix's domain as a rewrite (`bloommatrix.in/aira`, later `/anril`). A basePath makes every raw URL, PWA path and asset a trap (see the 2026-07-02 entries).
- **Decision (commit `d5815c2a`):** serve the app from the root of `https://www.anrilaitech.com` (Vercel project `anril-ai`). `basePath` removed; PWA manifest/scope, service worker, icons, push deep-links and in-app links use root paths. Backend CORS gained `anrilaitech.com` and `www.anrilaitech.com`; the legacy bloommatrix origins stay on purpose. The "open conversation" link points at the new domain. Legal/contact pages show the new website URL and the unused `aira@bloommatrix.in` address is gone (deletion requests go to the registered office).
- **Verified 2026-10-08:** `/` and `/login` on the new host return 200. Old `bloommatrix.in/anril` and `/anril/dashboard` return 404, `bloommatrix.in/aira` 308s to `/anril`. So old links are dead and nothing redirects them; see the backlog item.
- Details and the CORS test name: `subsystem-notes.md` -> "Domain: www.anrilaitech.com".

- **Why:** `next/font/google` fetches `fonts.googleapis.com` during `next build`; the Vercel build crashed with "Cannot read properties of null (reading '1')" when Google returned a font URL with no file extension.

CSS variable names (`--font-manrope`, `--font-mono`, `--font-heading`, ...) are unchanged, so no component changes. A new font means adding a woff2 file there, never `next/font/google`.

### 2026-10-12 — Inbox folders move into get_conversation_leads (migrations 223, 224)
- **Why:** the RPC paged across every conversation and `conversations.py` dropped other folders afterwards, so `total` was always the all-folders count (Blocked tab with 1 chat showed "Load more" against 697), Archived/Blocked only searched the 50 newest chats, Inbox "Load more" offsets drifted, and callers' own-leads filter had the same flaw.
- **Decision:** a 6-argument `get_conversation_leads(p_tenant_id, p_limit, p_offset, p_search, p_folder, p_caller_id)` applies folder + caller rule before COUNT/LIMIT/OFFSET; `conversations.py` passes both. The Inbox tab badge is read separately (`fetchConversations("chats", 1, 0)`) so it stays the Inbox count on other tabs.
- **223_conversation_leads_folder.sql — applied live 2026-10-12** (via Supabase MCP; verified Inbox 696 / Blocked 1 / Archived 0 on the busiest tenant). **224_drop_old_conversation_leads.sql — applied live 2026-10-12 by the user** in the SQL editor, after the backend with `p_folder` was confirmed on Render (its new `/share-product` route was live). 224 is not in `schema_migrations` because it ran in the editor; the old 4-arg function is gone (checked `pg_proc`).

### 2026-10-14 — Meta campaign creation date (migration 225)

### 2026-10-15 — Numbers pool reads Meta's portfolio limit (migration 226)
- **Why:** the pool showed 1,000/day for every number. Meta's limit moved to the business portfolio on 2025-10-07 (`whatsapp_business_manager_messaging_limit`), and our enum map keyed values Meta never sends. Astro Tamil was really at 2,000.
- **226_phone_numbers_meta_tier_values.sql — applied live 2026-10-15 via Supabase MCP** (before the backend that writes the new values). Widens `phone_numbers_messaging_tier_check` to `50, 250, 1000, 2000, 10000, 100000, 1000000` (1000000 = Unlimited).
- **Also decided:** Meta's "why is it red" text is read live (`GET /numbers/{id}/meta-health`), not stored, so there is no new column. Mechanics in [subsystem-notes](../context/subsystem-notes.md) → *Numbers pool: Meta messaging limits & quality*.

## 2026-10-16 — Meta Ads: multi-destination ads, join rule, client-picked history, All ads page

- **227_meta_ads_registry_and_tracking.sql — applied live 2026-10-16 via Supabase MCP** (before the backend that reads it; the report filters on `tracked_in_anril`, so the backend must not deploy without it). New `meta_ad_registry` (every Meta ad of any type, sticky `eligible`, admin-all + tenant-read RLS); `ad_creatives.destination_type` + `tracked_in_anril` (default true). Seeded the registry with existing creatives as eligible and stamped `meta_ads_connected_at` = apply time for the 4 tenants already connected, so nothing they saw disappears.
- **Decided (with the user):** Anril's WhatsApp report includes multi-destination ad sets (badged). Only ads running when the client connects, plus ads created after, are synced; paused/deleted later stays. The client picks how far back (one date, capped per ad by the ad's own start). Per-ad on/off switch, new ads ON. A separate All ads page shows every type with Meta's own numbers; the same Meta columns + breakdowns are optional on the WhatsApp report. Mechanics in [subsystem-notes](../context/subsystem-notes.md) → Meta Ads.

- **228_messaging_ads_channels.sql — applied live 2026-10-16 via Supabase MCP** (before the backend that writes `lead_meta_ad_attributions.channel` / reads `ad_creatives.messaging_channels` deploys). Adds `lead_meta_ad_attributions.channel` (whatsapp|instagram|messenger, default whatsapp) and `ad_creatives.messaging_channels text[]` backfilled from `destination_type`.
- **Decided (with the user, 2026-10-16):** the Meta Ads report becomes "Messaging ads" — every ad that opens WhatsApp, Instagram DM or Messenger (alone or mixed), with Anril counting chats on all three. Verification without a client running IG/Messenger ads: Meta's documented payloads end-to-end in-process (`tests/test_messaging_ads_end_to_end.py`) + a log line per ad referral for the first real click.

### 2026-10-16 — Number health from Meta only; failover redesigned (migration 229)
- **229_number_health_meta_status.sql — applied live 2026-10-16 via Supabase MCP** (additive; old backend still works). `phone_numbers.meta_status` / `meta_code_verification` (Meta's `status` / `code_verification_status`), `warm_up_started_at`, `rested_since`; `quality_rating` and `messaging_tier` lose their invented defaults (NULL = Meta hasn't reported); `leads.last_inbound_phone_number_id`; incident type `number_recovered`.
- **Decided (with the user):** a number sends or takes over only when Meta says CONNECTED (an Unverified/PENDING number shows "Unverified", never promoted, Set Primary blocked). Warm-up is 14 days from the first CONNECTED sync and is a broadcast cap (50→250/day), not a wall: a warming standby may take over. Red → the number rests (status `restricted`): no broadcasts, still replies to its own chats; recovers after 7 days once Meta shows Green, as a standby (does not retake primary). No usable standby → broadcasts pause ("Add a standby number"). Unsent broadcast recipients move to the new primary, or to 00:05 IST next day when every number is capped. The sync (12-hourly full pool sync + 4-hourly quality check), not only Meta's webhook, starts the switch. Daily counts reset at 00:00 IST (syncs no longer reset them). Astrologer replies go from the number the lead last messaged.
- **Removed:** the "we've moved to a new number" blast on failover — it sent from a day-0 number to every lead of the last 7 days, breaking warm-up caps, and the Red number keeps answering its own chats. Rules live in `app/services/number_health.py`; writes in `failover.py` / `number_sync.py`.

### 2026-10-17 — Count what Meta counts (migration 230)
- **230_template_send_log.sql — applied live 2026-10-17 via Supabase MCP** (before the backend that writes/reads it). New `template_send_log` (backend-only, RLS on, no policies) + `messaging_limit_usage(tenant)` → distinct recipients per number and for the business over the moving 24 hours.
- **Why:** Meta's limit is unique people sent to outside their 24h customer-service window, moving 24 hours, shared by the portfolio ([Meta docs](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp/messaging-limits)). `daily_send_count` counted every template and reset at midnight. Every template send (broadcasts, app triggers, alerts) now logs via `meta_cloud.send_template_message` → `outbound_router.count_template_send`, skipping recipients whose window with THAT number is open (`leads.last_inbound_phone_number_id`). App triggers are never limited, only counted (user decision); broadcasts + re-engagement templates are limited.
- **Also decided:** a capped broadcast's rest retries when the oldest counted send leaves the 24h window, held to 10:00–21:00 IST. The midnight `daily-send-reset` job is replaced by `send-log-prune` (03:00 IST). `phone_numbers.daily_send_count` / `last_reset_at` and the `increment_phone_daily_send_count` RPC are no longer used by the code.
- **231_drop_daily_send_count.sql — applied live 2026-10-17 by the user in the Supabase SQL editor (the MCP declines DROP COLUMN), after confirming the new backend was live** (the previous backend increments `daily_send_count` on every broadcast send). Drops `phone_numbers.daily_send_count`, `last_reset_at` and both `increment_phone_daily_send_count` overloads.
- **Follow-ups (2026-10-17, from Meta's messaging-limits doc):** recipients are normalised (`outbound_router.recipient_key`) so one person counts once; broadcasts stop at 90% of Meta's limit (`BROADCAST_SHARE_OF_LIMIT`, room for sends outside Anril; app triggers never stop); the deprecated `messaging_limit_tier` / `current_limit` / `max_daily_conversation_per_phone` are no longer read; the limit comes only from `max_daily_conversations_per_business` (sync field `whatsapp_business_manager_messaging_limit`, plus the `business_capability_update` and `phone_number_quality_update` webhooks) and is applied to every number of the tenant. An account-level webhook resolves its tenant from the WABA id only when exactly one tenant has that WABA. The Meta app must be subscribed to the `business_capability_update` webhook field.

### 2026-10-17 — Failed deliveries un-counted; templates editable when approved (migration 232)
- **232_template_send_log_message_id.sql — applied live 2026-10-17 via Supabase MCP.** `template_send_log.meta_message_id`; a `failed` status webhook deletes the row (Meta counts delivered messages only).
- **Templates:** editing allowed for APPROVED/REJECTED/PAUSED (Meta's rule: approved 1/24h, 10/30d, category fixed). The edit is pushed to Meta first; if Meta refuses, nothing changes locally (it used to save locally anyway and drift). `message_template_components_update` (edits made in WhatsApp Manager) now updates Anril's copy — URL/phone buttons from Meta, other buttons kept. Template webhooks (status + edits) are handled on the main WhatsApp webhook too: Meta sends every WABA field to the app's one callback URL, which serves inbound messages, so `/templates/webhook-status` alone likely never received them. Shared code: `templates.apply_template_change`.
- **Only APPROVED templates are used anywhere (user decision 2026-10-17):** added the missing checks to scheduled/drip broadcasts (PENDING → retry in an hour, anything else → fail with Meta's status), Send Now/scheduling (400), re-engagement (retry later, no log row) and the Astro reply-ready template (not sent). Auto-messages, marketplace, private send, call-details share, alerts and partner sends already checked. Every picker already listed only APPROVED. **233_template_status_values.sql — applied live 2026-10-17:** status check widened to DISABLED/ARCHIVED/DELETED; every Meta `message_template_status_update` event is mapped (REINSTATED→APPROVED, IN_APPEAL→PENDING, PENDING_DELETION→DELETED; FLAGGED/LOCKED/LIMIT_EXCEEDED/UNARCHIVED leave status alone), and both template syncs use the same mapping (`templates.stored_template_status`). Pre-check: all stored statuses matched Meta live (0 mismatches).
- **Template submit (2026-10-17):** a new template that doesn't reach Meta (refused, or Meta unreachable) is no longer saved — it used to be stored as REJECTED with no reason and no Meta id, and an edit then only changed Anril's copy, leaving it stuck PENDING forever. Now the error (Meta's own words) is shown and the form stays filled; a legacy row with no `meta_template_id` is submitted fresh when edited. Migration 231 still pending: the Supabase MCP declines DROP COLUMN; the user runs it in the SQL editor.
- **234_template_edit_log.sql — applied live 2026-10-17 via Supabase MCP.** Meta's approved-template edit limit (1 per 24h, 10 per 30 days) is enforced before Meta is called: `services/template_edit_limits.py`; every edit of an APPROVED template is logged (source `anril`, or `meta_webhook` for WhatsApp Manager edits reported by `message_template_components_update`; Meta's echo of Anril's own edit within 15 min is not double-counted). GET /templates adds `edit_allowance`; the Edit button is disabled with the reopen time when blocked. Meta's rule counts edits, not time since approval — a template approved today can be edited once.

### 2026-10-23 — Templates CSV export + fixed a page-scroll layout bug (`frontend/app/dashboard/templates/page.tsx`)
- **CSV export added** next to Sync/Carousel/Create Template on the templates list. Re-fetches templates fresh on click (so an approval since page-load is included, no stale cache) and exports whatever the current search/type/status filter shows — not hardcoded to APPROVED-only. Columns: Template Name, Anril ID, Content (header+body+footer+buttons+carousel cards flattened into one cell), Category, Language, Status. UTF-8 BOM prefix added so Tamil body text opens correctly in Excel.
- **Fixed a pre-existing (predates this session) mis-nested `</div>`:** the page's fixed-height/`overflow-hidden` wrapper (meant to lock stats+filters+list to one viewport, scrolling only the list internally) closed right after the stats/filter bar instead of after the scrollable list. The list then rendered as an unconstrained sibling further down the page, forcing the whole browser window to scroll through a dead blank gap to reach it. Moved the closing tag to after the list.
- **Scroll-position-on-return feature (sessionStorage-based) was built then explicitly reverted by the user same session** — not shipped. If asked again, don't assume the earlier approach was rejected for a technical reason; it was just a change of mind.

### 2026-10-25 — R&D runner moved from launchd to a Claude desktop scheduled task; reports leave the repo
- **Why launchd never worked:** 22 of its 25 nightly runs failed with "Operation not permitted" because macOS privacy protection (TCC) blocks a launchd-started `/bin/bash` from `~/Documents`. The last 3 failed with "No such file" after the folder was renamed from `Aira AI` to `anril AI`. The 2026-09-28 reports came from manual runs. The fix would have been Full Disk Access for `/bin/bash`, which opens the whole disk to every background bash script.
- **Now:** the Claude desktop scheduled task `anril-rnd-nightly` (cron `30 1 * * *`, saved in Auto mode, file `~/.claude/scheduled-tasks/anril-rnd-nightly/SKILL.md`) is a launcher only. It starts `scripts/rnd/run.sh auto` detached with `env -i`, so the runner doesn't inherit the session's ~35 `CLAUDE_*` variables (including a session-scoped `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`), and it inherits the app's Documents access. Tested 2026-10-25: a detached `env -i` process read `~/Documents` and ran `claude -p` after its parent exited. **Catch:** it runs only while the Claude app is open. If the app is closed at 01:30, it runs the next time the app opens. The launchd plist was unloaded and deleted.
- **Rename-proof fence:** `fence-settings.json` used to hard-code the checkout path, so after the rename its `.env` deny rules guarded a folder that no longer existed. It now says `__MAIN__`, and run.sh fills in the real path into `~/.aira-rnd/fence-settings.json` at start-up.
- **Reports** go to `~/Documents/anril-assets/rnd/reports` (`RND_OUT` overrides). `dashboard.py` reads from the same place. `.product-video/` also moved to `~/Documents/anril-assets/product-video`.

### 2026-10-28 — Schedule a template (migration 235, table `scheduled_template_sends`)
- **What:** wherever a template can be sent, staff can pick a time instead (Send now | Schedule, times in IST): the chat's Send template dialog, the Leads row/card dialog, the Telecalling Send details card, the New WhatsApp Message form, and the Leads bulk Send message (Template tab). The design was approved as a clickable mock-up first. Researched WATI / Interakt: only broadcasts/campaigns schedule; a per-contact scheduled template could not be confirmed for either.
- **Decided with the user:** when the 24 h window is open the dialog offers **Free message | Template** (free is the default); Schedule exists under Template only, because the window may have closed by send time. 5 minutes to 30 days ahead. A customer reply before send time does not stop it. Opted out / lead removed → `skipped`; Meta refusal or an unapproved template → `failed` + notification to whoever scheduled it. Cancel works until it is sent, with a 6 s Undo; at most 10 waiting per lead.
- **Not applied to the live database at commit time.** 235 must be applied BEFORE the backend deploy: the `scheduled-templates` job reads the table every minute and `GET /leads/{id}/scheduled-templates` fails without it. Additive and safe to apply first.
- **Known, unrelated:** `frontend/lib/brand-guard.test.ts` fails at HEAD on `LeadsClient.tsx:472` (`#25D366` channel dot).
